> [!warning] Conflict
> Flagged while digesting this document, not verified: Scope tag and country column are used inconsistently: Define Tax on Sales/Purchases Groups (102526) is tagged 'Global' yet lists specific countries (AT,AU,BE,...), its sibling Assign Company Codes to Tax on Sales/Purchases Groups (102521) is 'Country/Region Dependent' with a near-identical list, and Create/Assign Selection Variants (102238) is 'Country/Region Dependent' with country XX.
